Seed: 8 (replay this game with --seed=8)
The yellow player has four (04) pieces named Y1, Y2, Y3, and Y4.
The blue player has four (04) pieces named B1, B2, B3, and B4.
The red player has four (04) pieces named R1, R2, R3, and R4.
The green player has four (04) pieces named G1, G2, G3, and G4.
Yellow rolls 5
Blue rolls 5
Red rolls 5
Green rolls 2
Yellow player has the highest roll and will begin the game.
The order of a single round is Yellow, Blue, Red, and Green.

Yellow player rolled 1.
Yellow has no movable piece. Turn skipped.

Blue player rolled 3.
Blue has no movable piece. Turn skipped.

Red player rolled 4.
Red has no movable piece. Turn skipped.

Green player rolled 6.
Green player moves piece G1 to the starting point. The coin toss sets its direction to counterclockwise.
Green player now has 1/4 on pieces on the board and 3/4 pieces on the base.

Green player rolled 1.
Green moves piece G1 from location 39 to 38 by 1 units in counterclockwise direction.
Yellow player now has 0/4 on pieces on the board and 4/4 pieces on the base.
